`frontend/src/replay/draw.ts`:

```ts
import { toCanvas } from "../map/transform";
import type { Replay } from "../types";

const SIDE_COLOR = { blue: "#3b82f6", red: "#ef4444" } as const;
const TRAIL_SECONDS = 10;
const KILL_SHOW_SECONDS = 5;

function positionAt(track: [number, number, number][], t: number): [number, number, boolean] {
  const i = Math.max(0, Math.min(track.length - 1, Math.floor(t)));
  const j = Math.min(track.length - 1, i + 1);
  const u = t - i;
  const [x0, y0, a0] = track[i];
  const [x1, y1] = track[j];
  return [x0 + (x1 - x0) * u, y0 + (y1 - y0) * u, a0 === 1];
}

export function drawFrame(
  ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D,
  replay: Replay,
  t: number,
  size: number,
  map: HTMLImageElement | undefined,
  icons: Map<string, HTMLImageElement>,
) {
  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, size, size);
  if (map?.complete) ctx.drawImage(map, 0, 0, size, size);
  ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(9, 9, 11, 0.35)";
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, size, size);

  for (const w of replay.wards) {
    if (w.start > t || w.end <= t) continue;
    const [x, y] = toCanvas(w.x, w.y, size);
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(x, y, w.kind === "control" ? 4 : 3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fillStyle = w.kind === "control" ? "#e879f9" : SIDE_COLOR[w.side];
    ctx.fill();
  }

  for (const k of replay.kills) {
    if (k.t > t || t - k.t > KILL_SHOW_SECONDS) continue;
    const [x, y] = toCanvas(k.x, k.y, size);
    ctx.strokeStyle = SIDE_COLOR[k.side];
    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(x - 5, y - 5);
    ctx.lineTo(x + 5, y + 5);
    ctx.moveTo(x + 5, y - 5);
    ctx.lineTo(x - 5, y + 5);
    ctx.stroke();
  }

  const radius = Math.max(9, size / 40);
  for (const p of replay.participants) {
    const track = replay.positions[String(p.pid)];
    if (!track) continue;
    ctx.strokeStyle = `${SIDE_COLOR[p.side]}80`;
    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    ctx.beginPath();
    for (let s = Math.max(0, Math.floor(t - TRAIL_SECONDS)); s <= Math.floor(t); s++) {
      const [x, y] = toCanvas(track[s][0], track[s][1], size);
      if (s === Math.max(0, Math.floor(t - TRAIL_SECONDS))) ctx.moveTo(x, y);
      else ctx.lineTo(x, y);
    }
    ctx.stroke();

    const [gx, gy, alive] = positionAt(track, t);
    const [x, y] = toCanvas(gx, gy, size);
    ctx.save();
    ctx.globalAlpha = alive ? 1 : 0.3;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(x, y, radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.save();
    ctx.clip();
    const icon = icons.get(p.champion);
    if (icon?.complete) ctx.drawImage(icon, x - radius, y - radius, radius * 2, radius * 2);
    else {
      ctx.fillStyle = "#27272a";
      ctx.fill();
    }
    ctx.restore();
    ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
    ctx.strokeStyle = SIDE_COLOR[p.side];
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.restore();
  }
}
```

`frontend/src/replay/ReplayPlayer.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { api } from "../api";
import { useDdragon } from "../lib/ddragon";
import { fmtTime } from "../lib/time";
import type { Marker } from "../types";
import { advance, seek, type ClockState } from "./clock";
import { drawFrame } from "./draw";

const SPEEDS = [1, 2, 4, 8, 20];
const TONE = { good: "bg-emerald-400", bad: "bg-rose-400", neutral: "bg-zinc-400" } as const;

interface Props {
  gameId: string;
  start?: number;
  end?: number;
  markers?: Marker[];
}

export function ReplayPlayer({ gameId, start, end, markers = [] }: Props) {
  const { data: replay, error } = useQuery({ queryKey: ["replay", gameId], queryFn: () => api.replay(gameId) });
  const { iconFor, mapUrl } = useDdragon();
  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement>(null);
  const boxRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [size, setSize] = useState(560);
  const [clock, setClock] = useState<ClockState>({ t: 0, playing: false, speed: 2, start: 0, end: 1, loop: false });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!replay) return;
    const s = Math.max(0, start ?? 0);
    const e = Math.min(replay.game.duration, end ?? replay.game.duration);
    setClock({ t: s, playing: start !== undefined, speed: start !== undefined ? 1 : 4, start: s, end: e,
      loop: start !== undefined });
  }, [replay, start, end]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const box = boxRef.current;
    if (!box) return;
    const observer = new ResizeObserver(([entry]) => setSize(Math.min(640, Math.floor(entry.contentRect.width))));
    observer.observe(box);
    return () => observer.disconnect();
  }, []);

  const images = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Image();
    map.crossOrigin = "anonymous";
    map.src = mapUrl;
    const icons = new Map<string, HTMLImageElement>();
    for (const p of replay?.participants ?? []) {
      const url = iconFor(p.champion);
      if (!url) continue;
      const img = new Image();
      img.crossOrigin = "anonymous";
      img.src = url;
      icons.set(p.champion, img);
    }
    return { map, icons };
  }, [replay, mapUrl, iconFor]);

  useEffect(() => {
    let frame = 0;
    let last = performance.now();
    const loop = (now: number) => {
      setClock((c) => advance(c, now - last));
      last = now;
      frame = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
    };
    frame = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const ctx = canvasRef.current?.getContext("2d");
    if (ctx && replay) drawFrame(ctx, replay, clock.t, size, images.map, images.icons);
  }, [clock.t, replay, size, images]);

  if (error) return <p className="text-rose-400">Could not load the replay.</p>;
  if (!replay) return <div className="aspect-square w-full max-w-[640px] animate-pulse rounded bg-zinc-900" />;

  const span = clock.end - clock.start || 1;
  return (
    <div ref={boxRef} className="w-full max-w-[640px]">
      <canvas ref={canvasRef} width={size} height={size} className="rounded" />
      <div className="mt-3 flex items-center gap-3 text-sm">
        <button
          type="button"
          className="rounded bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-1 hover:bg-zinc-700"
          onClick={() => setClock((c) => ({ ...c, playing: !c.playing, t: c.t >= c.end ? c.start : c.t }))}
        >
          {clock.playing ? "Pause" : "Play"}
        </button>
        <select
          className="rounded bg-zinc-800 px-2 py-1"
          value={clock.speed}
          onChange={(e) => setClock((c) => ({ ...c, speed: Number(e.target.value) }))}
        >
          {SPEEDS.map((s) => (
            <option key={s} value={s}>{s}x</option>
          ))}
        </select>
        <span className="tabular-nums text-zinc-400">{fmtTime(clock.t)} / {fmtTime(clock.end)}</span>
      </div>
      <div className="relative mt-2">
        <input
          type="range"
          min={clock.start}
          max={clock.end}
          step={0.5}
          value={clock.t}
          onChange={(e) => setClock((c) => seek(c, Number(e.target.value)))}
          className="w-full"
          aria-label="Game time"
        />
        {markers
          .filter((m) => m.t >= clock.start && m.t <= clock.end)
          .map((m) => (
            <button
              key={`${m.t}-${m.label}`}
              type="button"
              title={`${fmtTime(m.t)} ${m.label}`}
              onClick={() => setClock((c) => seek(c, m.t))}
              className={`absolute -top-1 h-2 w-2 rounded-full ${TONE[m.tone]}`}
              style={{ left: `${((m.t - clock.start) / span) * 100}%` }}
            />
          ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { api } from "../api";

export function HomePage() {
  const [q, setQ] = useState("");
  const { data: games = [], isLoading } = useQuery({ queryKey: ["games", q], queryFn: () => api.games(q) });
  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-4xl px-4 py-8">
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold">Throughline</h1>
      <p className="mt-1 text-zinc-400">Did we play our draft?</p>
      <div className="mt-6 flex gap-3">
        <input
          value={q}
          onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="Search a team, a tournament or a champion"
          className="flex-1 rounded bg-zinc-900 px-3 py-2"
        />
        <Link to="/plan/new" className="rounded bg-zinc-100 px-4 py-2 font-medium text-zinc-900">New draft</Link>
      </div>
      {isLoading && <p className="mt-6 text-zinc-500">Loading games...</p>}
      <ul className="mt-6 divide-y divide-zinc-800">
        {games.map((g) => (
          <li key={g.game_id} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 py-3">
            <div>
              <div className="font-medium">
                <span className="text-blue-side">{g.teams.blue}</span> vs <span className="text-red-side">{g.teams.red}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="text-xs text-zinc-500">
                {g.tournament} · patch {g.patch} · {new Date(g.started_at).toLocaleDateString()}
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 text-sm">
              {(["blue", "red"] as const).map((side) => (
                <Link key={side} to={`/plan/from-game/${encodeURIComponent(g.game_id)}?us=${side}`}
                  className="rounded bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-1">
                  Plan as {g.teams[side]}
                </Link>
              ))}
              <Link to={`/replay/${encodeURIComponent(g.game_id)}`} className="rounded bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-1">Replay</Link>
            </div>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
    </main>
  );
}
```

`backend/throughline/knowledge/items.py`:

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import httpx

DDRAGON = "https://ddragon.leagueoflegends.com"
LEGENDARY_MIN_GOLD = 2200
EXCLUDED_TAGS = {"Boots", "Consumable", "Trinket"}


def legendary_item_ids(item_json: dict[str, Any]) -> set[int]:
    out: set[int] = set()
    for key, item in item_json["data"].items():
        gold = item.get("gold", {})
        if not item.get("maps", {}).get("11", False):
            continue
        if not gold.get("purchasable", False) or gold.get("total", 0) < LEGENDARY_MIN_GOLD:
            continue
        if item.get("into") or EXCLUDED_TAGS & set(item.get("tags", [])):
            continue
        out.add(int(key))
    return out


def fetch_item_json(cache_dir: Path, http: httpx.Client | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    client = http or httpx.Client(timeout=30.0)
    version = client.get(f"{DDRAGON}/api/versions.json").json()[0]
    path = cache_dir / f"items-{version}.json"
    if path.exists():
        cached: dict[str, Any] = json.loads(path.read_text())
        return cached
    body: dict[str, Any] = client.get(f"{DDRAGON}/cdn/{version}/data/en_US/item.json").json()
    cache_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path.write_text(json.dumps(body))
    return body
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement features**

`backend/throughline/knowledge/features.py`:

```python
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime

from throughline.game.index import GameIndex
from throughline.game.model import Game, ObjectiveKind, Role, Side, other

CURVE_MINUTES = (5, 10, 15, 20, 25, 30)
SPIKE_AFTER = 180.0
ROLE_GD_10 = 600.0
ROLE_GD_14 = 840.0


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ParticipantFeatures:
    game_id: str
    started_at: datetime
    patch: str
    side: Side
    role: Role
    champion: str
    opponent: str
    won: bool
    team_gd: dict[int, int | None]
    role_gd10: int | None
    role_gd14: int | None
    level6_at: float | None
    item1_at: float | None
    item2_at: float | None
    gd_after_level6: int | None
    gd_after_item1: int | None
    first_dragon: bool | None
    first_grubs: bool | None
    first_herald: bool | None
    first_baron: bool | None


def _first_taker(game: Game, kind: ObjectiveKind) -> Side | None:
    taken = next((o for o in game.objectives if o.kind == kind), None)
    return taken.by if taken else None


def participant_features(game: Game, legendary: set[int]) -> list[ParticipantFeatures]:
    index = GameIndex(game)
    duration = game.duration

    def gd_after(side: Side, t: float | None) -> int | None:
        if t is None or t + SPIKE_AFTER > duration:
            return None
        return index.gold_diff(side, t + SPIKE_AFTER) - index.gold_diff(side, t)

    takers = {kind: _first_taker(game, kind) for kind in ("dragon", "grubs", "herald", "baron")}
    rows: list[ParticipantFeatures] = []
    for p in game.participants:
        opponent = game.player(other(p.side), p.role)
        level6 = next((f.t for f in game.frames if f.pid == p.pid and f.level >= 6), None)
        bought: list[float] = []
        seen: set[int] = set()
        for purchase in game.items:
            if purchase.pid == p.pid and purchase.item_id in legendary and purchase.item_id not in seen:
                seen.add(purchase.item_id)
                bought.append(purchase.t)
        item1 = bought[0] if bought else None
        rows.append(ParticipantFeatures(
            game_id=game.meta.game_id,
            started_at=game.meta.started_at,
            patch=game.patch,
            side=p.side,
            role=p.role,
            champion=p.champion,
            opponent=opponent.champion,
            won=game.winner == p.side,
            team_gd={m: index.gold_diff(p.side, m * 60.0) if m * 60.0 <= duration else None
                     for m in CURVE_MINUTES},
            role_gd10=index.role_gold_diff(p.side, p.role, ROLE_GD_10) if duration >= ROLE_GD_10 else None,
            role_gd14=index.role_gold_diff(p.side, p.role, ROLE_GD_14) if duration >= ROLE_GD_14 else None,
            level6_at=level6,
            item1_at=item1,
            item2_at=bought[1] if len(bought) > 1 else None,
            gd_after_level6=gd_after(p.side, level6),
            gd_after_item1=gd_after(p.side, item1),
            first_dragon=None if takers["dragon"] is None else takers["dragon"] == p.side,
            first_grubs=None if takers["grubs"] is None else takers["grubs"] == p.side,
            first_herald=None if takers["herald"] is None else takers["herald"] == p.side,
            first_baron=None if takers["baron"] is None else takers["baron"] == p.side,
        ))
    return rows
```

Append to `SCHEMA_SQL` in `backend/throughline/store/analytics.py`:

```sql
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS participant_features (
  game_id TEXT, started_at TIMESTAMPTZ, patch TEXT, side TEXT, role TEXT, champion TEXT, opponent TEXT,
  won BOOLEAN, gd5 INTEGER, gd10 INTEGER, gd15 INTEGER, gd20 INTEGER, gd25 INTEGER, gd30 INTEGER,
```

```python
from throughline.knowledge.features import participant_features
from throughline.knowledge.items import fetch_item_json, legendary_item_ids


@app.command()
def build() -> None:
    """Compute participant features for every stored game."""
    settings = Settings.from_env()
    store = AnalyticsStore(settings.analytics_db)
    store.init_schema()
    legendary = legendary_item_ids(fetch_item_json(settings.data_dir / "ddragon"))
    ids = store.game_ids_by_time()
    for n, game_id in enumerate(ids, start=1):
        store.write_features(game_id, participant_features(store.load_game(game_id), legendary))
        if n % 50 == 0 or n == len(ids):
            typer.echo(f"{n}/{len(ids)} games")
```

```python
from pathlib import Path

from throughline.knowledge.base import KnowledgeBase, patch_key, shrink
from throughline.knowledge.features import CURVE_MINUTES, ParticipantFeatures
from throughline.store.analytics import AnalyticsStore

T0 = datetime(2026, 10, 1, tzinfo=UTC)


def row(n: int, **overrides: object) -> ParticipantFeatures:
    base = ParticipantFeatures(
        game_id=f"g{n}", started_at=T0 + timedelta(days=n), patch="15.23", side="blue", role="mid",
        champion="Ahri", opponent="Orianna", won=True, team_gd={m: 0 for m in CURVE_MINUTES},
        role_gd10=0, role_gd14=0, level6_at=400.0, item1_at=800.0, item2_at=None,
        gd_after_level6=0, gd_after_item1=0, first_dragon=None, first_grubs=None, first_herald=None,
        first_baron=None)
    return replace(base, **overrides)  # type: ignore[arg-type]


@pytest.fixture

    s = AnalyticsStore(tmp_path / "a.duckdb")
    s.init_schema()
    return s


def write(store: AnalyticsStore, rows: list[ParticipantFeatures]) -> None:
    for r in rows:
        store.write_features(r.game_id + r.side + r.role, [r])


def test_shrink_and_patch_key() -> None:
    assert shrink(500.0, 20) == 250.0
    assert shrink(500.0, 0) == 0.0
    assert patch_key("15.9") < patch_key("15.10")


def test_curve_is_shrunk_by_sample(store: AnalyticsStore) -> None:
    write(store, [row(n, team_gd={m: 500 for m in CURVE_MINUTES}) for n in range(20)])
    kb = KnowledgeBase(store, "15.23", min_scope_games=1)
    stat = kb.curve("mid", "Ahri")[10]
    assert (stat.value, stat.games) == (250.0, 20)
    assert kb.curve("mid", "Zed")[10].games == 0


def test_patch_window_and_time_split(store: AnalyticsStore) -> None:
    rows = [row(1, patch="15.20", role_gd10=1000), row(2, patch="15.21", role_gd10=100),
            row(3, patch="15.22", role_gd10=100), row(4, patch="15.23", role_gd10=100),
            row(5, patch="15.23", role_gd10=100), row(6, patch="15.24", role_gd10=100)]
    write(store, rows)
    kb = KnowledgeBase(store, "15.23", before=T0 + timedelta(days=5), min_scope_games=1)
    assert kb.patches == ["15.21", "15.22", "15.23"]
    assert kb.scope_games == 3  # g2, g3, g4: g5 is not before day 5, g1 is outside the window
    fallback = KnowledgeBase(store, "15.23", min_scope_games=100)
    assert fallback.patches == []
    assert fallback.scope_games == 6


def test_matchup_needs_enough_games(store: AnalyticsStore) -> None:
    write(store, [row(n, role_gd10=300) for n in range(4)])
    kb = KnowledgeBase(store, "15.23", min_scope_games=1)
    assert kb.matchup("mid", "Ahri", "Orianna") is None
    write(store, [row(10 + n, role_gd10=300) for n in range(2)])
    kb = KnowledgeBase(store, "15.23", min_scope_games=1)
    matchup = kb.matchup("mid", "Ahri", "Orianna")
    assert matchup is not None and (matchup.value, matchup.games) == (300.0, 6)


def test_spike_and_tendency(store: AnalyticsStore) -> None:
    write(store, [row(n, level6_at=380.0 + n, gd_after_level6=400, first_dragon=n % 4 != 0)
                  for n in range(20)])
    kb = KnowledgeBase(store, "15.23", min_scope_games=1)
    spike = kb.spike("mid", "Ahri", "level6")
    assert spike is not None
    assert spike.at == pytest.approx(389.5)
    assert spike.gd_after == pytest.approx(200.0)
    assert kb.tendency("Ahri", "dragon").value == pytest.approx(shrink(0.75 - 0.5, 20))
    assert kb.spike("mid", "Zed", "level6") is None
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`backend/throughline/knowledge/base.py`:

```python
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Any, Literal, Protocol

from throughline.game.model import Role
from throughline.knowledge.features import CURVE_MINUTES
from throughline.store.analytics import AnalyticsStore

PRIOR_GAMES = 20.0
MIN_MATCHUP_GAMES = 5
MIN_SPIKE_GAMES = 10
PATCH_WINDOW = 3
MIN_SCOPE_GAMES = 200

SpikeKind = Literal["level6", "item1"]
TendencyKind = Literal["dragon", "grubs", "herald", "baron"]
SPIKE_COLUMNS: dict[SpikeKind, tuple[str, str]] = {
    "level6": ("level6_at", "gd_after_level6"),
    "item1": ("item1_at", "gd_after_item1"),
}


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Stat:
    value: float
    games: int


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Spike:
    at: float
    gd_after: float
    games: int


def shrink(mean: float, n: int, k: float = PRIOR_GAMES) -> float:
    """Pull a mean toward zero by its sample size: n games weigh against k games of 'no effect'."""
    return 0.0 if n == 0 else mean * n / (n + k)


def patch_key(patch: str) -> tuple[int, int]:
    major, _, minor = patch.partition(".")
    try:
        return int(major), int(minor)
    except ValueError:
        return (0, 0)


class KnowledgeSource(Protocol):
    scope_games: int

    def curve(self, role: Role, champion: str) -> dict[int, Stat]: ...
    def role_edge(self, role: Role, champion: str) -> Stat: ...
    def matchup(self, role: Role, champion: str, opponent: str) -> Stat | None: ...
    def spike(self, role: Role, champion: str, kind: SpikeKind) -> Spike | None: ...
    def tendency(self, champion: str, objective: TendencyKind) -> Stat: ...


class KnowledgeBase:
    """Aggregates over participant_features, limited to a patch window and to games before a time."""

    def __init__(self, store: AnalyticsStore, patch: str, before: datetime | None = None,
                 min_scope_games: int = MIN_SCOPE_GAMES) -> None:
        self.con = store.con
        time_sql, time_params = ("started_at < ?", [before]) if before else ("TRUE", [])
        known = sorted(
            {r[0] for r in self.con.execute(
                f"SELECT DISTINCT patch FROM participant_features WHERE {time_sql}", time_params).fetchall()
             if r[0]},
            key=patch_key,
        )
        window = [p for p in known if patch_key(p) <= patch_key(patch)][-PATCH_WINDOW:]
        self.patches = window
        self._where, self._params = self._scope(time_sql, time_params, window)
        self.scope_games = self._count_games()
        if self.scope_games < min_scope_games:
            self.patches = []
            self._where, self._params = self._scope(time_sql, time_params, [])
            self.scope_games = self._count_games()

    @staticmethod
    def _scope(time_sql: str, time_params: list[Any], patches: list[str]) -> tuple[str, list[Any]]:
        if not patches:
            return time_sql, list(time_params)
        marks = ", ".join("?" for _ in patches)
        return f"{time_sql} AND patch IN ({marks})", [*time_params, *patches]

    def _count_games(self) -> int:
        row = self.con.execute(
            f"SELECT count(DISTINCT game_id) FROM participant_features WHERE {self._where}", self._params
        ).fetchone()
        return int(row[0]) if row else 0

    def _one(self, select: str, where: str, params: list[Any]) -> tuple[Any, ...]:
        row = self.con.execute(
            f"SELECT {select} FROM participant_features WHERE {self._where} AND {where}", [*self._params, *params]
        ).fetchone()
        assert row is not None
        return tuple(row)

    def curve(self, role: Role, champion: str) -> dict[int, Stat]:
        select = ", ".join(f"avg(gd{m}), count(gd{m})" for m in CURVE_MINUTES)
        row = self._one(select, "role = ? AND champion = ?", [role, champion])
        out: dict[int, Stat] = {}
        for i, m in enumerate(CURVE_MINUTES):
            mean, n = row[2 * i], int(row[2 * i + 1])
            out[m] = Stat(shrink(float(mean or 0.0), n), n)
        return out

    def role_edge(self, role: Role, champion: str) -> Stat:
        mean, n = self._one("avg(role_gd10), count(role_gd10)", "role = ? AND champion = ?", [role, champion])
        return Stat(shrink(float(mean or 0.0), int(n)), int(n))

    def matchup(self, role: Role, champion: str, opponent: str) -> Stat | None:
        mean, n = self._one("avg(role_gd10), count(role_gd10)", "role = ? AND champion = ? AND opponent = ?",
                            [role, champion, opponent])
        return Stat(float(mean), int(n)) if int(n) >= MIN_MATCHUP_GAMES else None

    def spike(self, role: Role, champion: str, kind: SpikeKind) -> Spike | None:
        at_col, gd_col = SPIKE_COLUMNS[kind]
        at, gd, n = self._one(f"median({at_col}), avg({gd_col}), count({gd_col})",
                              f"role = ? AND champion = ? AND {at_col} IS NOT NULL", [role, champion])
        if int(n) < MIN_SPIKE_GAMES or at is None:
            return None
        return Spike(float(at), shrink(float(gd or 0.0), int(n)), int(n))

    def tendency(self, champion: str, objective: TendencyKind) -> Stat:
        column = f"first_{objective}"
        rate, n = self._one(f"avg(CASE WHEN {column} THEN 1.0 ELSE 0.0 END), count({column})",
                            f"champion = ? AND {column} IS NOT NULL", [champion])
        return Stat(shrink(float(rate) - 0.5, int(n)) if rate is not None else 0.0, int(n))
```

```python
"""Every threshold the plan generator uses. Tuned on the earlier, held-out split only (see validation)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from throughline.game.model import Lane

WINDOW_GOLD = 300.0          # draft tempo beyond this is a window
LANE_GOLD = 150.0            # lane edge at 10:00 beyond this is priority
SPIKE_GOLD = 250.0           # team gold gained in the 3 minutes after a spike
TENDENCY_UNIT = 0.10         # objective-rate edge that counts as one unit of signal
OBJECTIVE_SIGNAL = 1.0       # signal needed to contest (or concede) an objective
MIN_ITEM_GAMES = 10          # every piece of evidence needs this many games
CONFIDENT_GAMES = 50         # games at which an item's score is no longer discounted
MAX_ITEMS = 8
LANE_UNTIL = 480.0
SIDE_UNTIL = 360.0
SIDE_WEIGHT = 0.8
OBJECTIVE_LEAD = 20.0        # be set up this long before the spawn
OBJECTIVE_MIN_PLAYERS = 3
OBJECTIVE_MIN_WARDS = 2

PLAN_OBJECTIVES = ("dragon", "grubs", "herald")
OBJECTIVE_LANES: dict[str, tuple[Lane, ...]] = {"dragon": ("bot",), "grubs": ("top",), "herald": ("top", "mid")}
TRADE_FALLBACK: dict[str, list[Lane]] = {"dragon": ["top"], "grubs": ["bot"], "herald": ["bot"]}
OBJECTIVE_NAMES = {"dragon": "first dragon", "grubs": "void grubs", "herald": "Rift Herald"}
```

`backend/throughline/plan/text.py`:

```python
from __future__ import annotations


def fmt_time(seconds: float) -> str:
    total = max(0, int(seconds))
    return f"{total // 60}:{total % 60:02d}"


def fmt_gold(value: float) -> str:
    rounded = round(value)
    if rounded == 0:
        return "0"
    return f"{'+' if rounded > 0 else '-'}{abs(rounded):,}"
```

`backend/throughline/plan/models.py`:

```python
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Annotated, Literal

from pydantic import BaseModel, Field, model_validator

from throughline.game.model import ROLES, Lane, Role, Side

Owner = Literal["us", "them"]
PlanObjective = Literal["dragon", "grubs", "herald"]
Origin = Literal["generated", "edited", "manual"]


class Evidence(BaseModel):
    text: str
    games: int


class ItemBase(BaseModel):
    id: str
    text: str
    evidence: list[Evidence] = []
    score: float = 0.0
    origin: Origin = "generated"


class ObjectiveItem(ItemBase):
    type: Literal["objective"] = "objective"
    objective: PlanObjective
    spawn_at: float
    by: float
    min_players: int = 3
    min_wards: int = 2


class ConcedeItem(ItemBase):
    type: Literal["concede"] = "concede"
    objective: PlanObjective
    spawn_at: float
    trade_lanes: list[Lane]


class LaneItem(ItemBase):
    type: Literal["lane"] = "lane"
    lane: Lane
    until: float


class WindowItem(ItemBase):
    type: Literal["window"] = "window"
    stance: Literal["force", "avoid"]
    start: float
    end: float


class SpikeItem(ItemBase):
    type: Literal["spike"] = "spike"
    enemy_champion: str
    role: Role
    kind: Literal["level6", "item1"]
    at: float


class SideItem(ItemBase):
    type: Literal["side"] = "side"
    half: Literal["top", "bot"]
    until: float


PlanItem = Annotated[
    ObjectiveItem | ConcedeItem | LaneItem | WindowItem | SpikeItem | SideItem,
    Field(discriminator="type"),
]


def item_time(item: PlanItem) -> float:
    match item:
        case ObjectiveItem() | ConcedeItem():
            return item.spawn_at
        case WindowItem():
            return item.start
        case SpikeItem():
            return item.at
        case _:
            return 0.0


class Draft(BaseModel):
    blue: dict[Role, str]
    red: dict[Role, str]
    us: Side
    patch: str | None = None

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def _five_roles(self) -> Draft:
        for side in (self.blue, self.red):
            if set(side) != set(ROLES) or not all(side.values()):
                raise ValueError("each side needs a champion for top, jungle, mid, bot and support")
        return self

    def ours(self) -> dict[Role, str]:
        return self.blue if self.us == "blue" else self.red

    def theirs(self) -> dict[Role, str]:
        return self.red if self.us == "blue" else self.blue


class TempoPoint(BaseModel):
    minute: int
    value: float
    games: int


class Window(BaseModel):
    start: float
    end: float
    owner: Owner
    peak: float
    games: int


class LanePriority(BaseModel):
    lane: Lane
    owner: Owner | None
    value: float
    games: int
    detail: str


class Plan(BaseModel):
    draft: Draft
    items: list[PlanItem]
    tempo: list[TempoPoint]
    windows: list[Window]
    lanes: list[LanePriority]
    scope_games: int
```

```python
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field

from throughline.game.model import Role
from throughline.knowledge.base import Spike, SpikeKind, Stat, TendencyKind
from throughline.knowledge.features import CURVE_MINUTES


@dataclass
class FakeKnowledge:
    games: int = 60
    scope_games: int = 600
    curves: dict[str, dict[int, float]] = field(default_factory=dict)   # champion -> minute -> gold
    edges: dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)              # champion -> role gd at 10
    matchups: dict[tuple[str, str], float] = field(default_factory=dict)
    spikes: dict[tuple[str, SpikeKind], tuple[float, float]] = field(default_factory=dict)  # (at, gd_after)
    tendencies: dict[tuple[str, TendencyKind], float] = field(default_factory=dict)

    def curve(self, role: Role, champion: str) -> dict[int, Stat]:
        values = self.curves.get(champion, {})
        return {m: Stat(values.get(m, 0.0), self.games) for m in CURVE_MINUTES}

    def role_edge(self, role: Role, champion: str) -> Stat:
        return Stat(self.edges.get(champion, 0.0), self.games)

    def matchup(self, role: Role, champion: str, opponent: str) -> Stat | None:
        value = self.matchups.get((champion, opponent))
        return Stat(value, self.games) if value is not None else None

    def spike(self, role: Role, champion: str, kind: SpikeKind) -> Spike | None:
        found = self.spikes.get((champion, kind))
        return Spike(found[0], found[1], self.games) if found else None

    def tendency(self, champion: str, objective: TendencyKind) -> Stat:
        return Stat(self.tendencies.get((champion, objective), 0.0), self.games)
```

```python
import pytest

from tests.plan.fake_knowledge import FakeKnowledge
from throughline.plan.models import Draft
from throughline.plan.tempo import draft_tempo, lane_priorities, tempo_at, windows

BLUE = {"top": "Aatrox", "jungle": "Vi", "mid": "Ahri", "bot": "Ezreal", "support": "Rakan"}
RED = {"top": "Gnar", "jungle": "Sejuani", "mid": "Orianna", "bot": "Kaisa", "support": "Nautilus"}
DRAFT = Draft(blue=BLUE, red=RED, us="blue", patch="15.23")


def test_tempo_is_mean_of_ours_minus_mean_of_theirs() -> None:
    kb = FakeKnowledge(curves={"Vi": {5: 1000.0}, "Sejuani": {5: 500.0}})
    points = draft_tempo(kb, DRAFT)
    assert points[0].minute == 5
    assert points[0].value == pytest.approx(100.0)  # (1000 - 500) / 5
    assert points[0].games == 60


def test_tempo_at_interpolates_from_zero() -> None:
    kb = FakeKnowledge(curves={c: {5: 500.0, 10: 1000.0} for c in BLUE.values()})
    points = draft_tempo(kb, DRAFT)
    assert tempo_at(points, 150.0) == pytest.approx(250.0)
    assert tempo_at(points, 450.0) == pytest.approx(750.0)
    assert tempo_at(points, 5000.0) == pytest.approx(0.0)


def test_windows_merge_and_take_owner() -> None:
    kb = FakeKnowledge(curves={
        **{c: {5: 400.0, 10: 400.0} for c in BLUE.values()},
        **{c: {25: 400.0, 30: 400.0} for c in RED.values()},
    })
    found = windows(draft_tempo(kb, DRAFT))
    assert [(w.start, w.end, w.owner) for w in found] == [(150.0, 750.0, "us"), (1350.0, 1950.0, "them")]
    assert found[0].peak == pytest.approx(400.0)


def test_lane_priorities_prefer_matchups() -> None:
    kb = FakeKnowledge(edges={"Ezreal": 400.0, "Rakan": 0.0, "Aatrox": 100.0},
                       matchups={("Ahri", "Orianna"): -200.0})
    lanes = lane_priorities(kb, DRAFT)
    assert (lanes["bot"].owner, lanes["bot"].value) == ("us", 200.0)
    assert lanes["top"].owner is None
    assert (lanes["mid"].owner, lanes["mid"].value) == ("them", -200.0)
    assert "Ahri vs Orianna" in lanes["mid"].detail
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`backend/throughline/plan/tempo.py`:

```python
from __future__ import annotations

from statistics import mean

from throughline.game.model import LANE_ROLES, LANES, Lane, Role
from throughline.knowledge.base import KnowledgeSource
from throughline.knowledge.features import CURVE_MINUTES
from throughline.plan.constants import LANE_GOLD, WINDOW_GOLD
from throughline.plan.models import Draft, LanePriority, Owner, TempoPoint, Window
from throughline.plan.text import fmt_gold

WINDOW_HALF_WIDTH = 150.0  # each tempo point stands for 2.5 minutes either side


def draft_tempo(kb: KnowledgeSource, draft: Draft) -> list[TempoPoint]:
    ours = [kb.curve(role, champion) for role, champion in draft.ours().items()]
    theirs = [kb.curve(role, champion) for role, champion in draft.theirs().items()]
    points: list[TempoPoint] = []
    for m in CURVE_MINUTES:
        value = mean(c[m].value for c in ours) - mean(c[m].value for c in theirs)
        games = min(c[m].games for c in ours + theirs)
        points.append(TempoPoint(minute=m, value=round(value, 1), games=games))
    return points


def tempo_at(points: list[TempoPoint], t: float) -> float:
    xs = [0.0] + [p.minute * 60.0 for p in points]
    ys = [0.0] + [p.value for p in points]
    if t >= xs[-1]:
        return ys[-1]
    for i in range(1, len(xs)):
        if t <= xs[i]:
            u = (t - xs[i - 1]) / (xs[i] - xs[i - 1])
            return ys[i - 1] + (ys[i] - ys[i - 1]) * u
    return ys[-1]


def tempo_games_at(points: list[TempoPoint], t: float) -> int:
    nearest = min(points, key=lambda p: abs(p.minute * 60.0 - t))
    return nearest.games


def windows(points: list[TempoPoint]) -> list[Window]:
    out: list[Window] = []
    for p in points:
        if abs(p.value) < WINDOW_GOLD:
            continue
        owner: Owner = "us" if p.value > 0 else "them"
        start, end = p.minute * 60.0 - WINDOW_HALF_WIDTH, p.minute * 60.0 + WINDOW_HALF_WIDTH
        last = out[-1] if out else None
        if last and last.owner == owner and last.end >= start:
            out[-1] = Window(start=last.start, end=end, owner=owner,
                             peak=max(last.peak, abs(p.value)), games=min(last.games, p.games))
        else:
            out.append(Window(start=max(0.0, start), end=end, owner=owner, peak=abs(p.value), games=p.games))
    return out


def _role_value(kb: KnowledgeSource, draft: Draft, role: Role) -> tuple[float, int, str]:
    ours, theirs = draft.ours()[role], draft.theirs()[role]
    matchup = kb.matchup(role, ours, theirs)
    if matchup is not None:
        return (matchup.value, matchup.games,
                f"{ours} vs {theirs}: {fmt_gold(matchup.value)} gold at 10:00 ({matchup.games} games)")
    a, b = kb.role_edge(role, ours), kb.role_edge(role, theirs)
    games = min(a.games, b.games)
    return (a.value - b.value, games,
            f"{ours} {fmt_gold(a.value)} vs {theirs} {fmt_gold(b.value)} gold at 10:00 on average ({games} games)")


def lane_priorities(kb: KnowledgeSource, draft: Draft) -> dict[Lane, LanePriority]:
    out: dict[Lane, LanePriority] = {}
    for lane in LANES:
        parts = [_role_value(kb, draft, role) for role in LANE_ROLES[lane]]
        value = mean(p[0] for p in parts)
        owner: Owner | None = None
        if abs(value) >= LANE_GOLD:
            owner = "us" if value > 0 else "them"
        out[lane] = LanePriority(lane=lane, owner=owner, value=round(value, 1),
                                 games=min(p[1] for p in parts), detail="; ".join(p[2] for p in parts))
    return out
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`backend/throughline/plan/generator.py`:

```python
from __future__ import annotations

from statistics import mean

from throughline.game.model import ROLE_LANE, Lane, Role
from throughline.game.setups import SPAWNS
from throughline.knowledge.base import KnowledgeSource
from throughline.plan.constants import (
    CONFIDENT_GAMES,
    LANE_GOLD,
    LANE_UNTIL,
    MAX_ITEMS,
    MIN_ITEM_GAMES,
    OBJECTIVE_LANES,
    OBJECTIVE_LEAD,
    OBJECTIVE_MIN_PLAYERS,
    OBJECTIVE_MIN_WARDS,
    OBJECTIVE_NAMES,
    OBJECTIVE_SIGNAL,
    PLAN_OBJECTIVES,
    SIDE_UNTIL,
    SIDE_WEIGHT,
    SPIKE_GOLD,
    TENDENCY_UNIT,
    TRADE_FALLBACK,
    WINDOW_GOLD,
)
from throughline.plan.models import (
    ConcedeItem,
    Draft,
    Evidence,
    LaneItem,
    LanePriority,
    ObjectiveItem,
    Plan,
    PlanItem,
    SideItem,
    SpikeItem,
    TempoPoint,
    Window,
    WindowItem,
    item_time,
)
from throughline.plan.tempo import draft_tempo, lane_priorities, tempo_at, tempo_games_at, windows
from throughline.plan.text import fmt_gold, fmt_time

SPIKE_ROLES: tuple[Role, ...] = ("top", "mid", "bot")


def confidence(games: int) -> float:
    return min(1.0, games / CONFIDENT_GAMES)


def _objective_items(kb: KnowledgeSource, draft: Draft, tempo: list[TempoPoint],
                     lanes: dict[Lane, LanePriority]) -> list[PlanItem]:
    items: list[PlanItem] = []
    for objective in PLAN_OBJECTIVES:
        spawn = SPAWNS[objective][0]
        name = OBJECTIVE_NAMES[objective]
        lane_list = OBJECTIVE_LANES[objective]
        lane_value = mean(lanes[lane].value for lane in lane_list)
        lane_games = min(lanes[lane].games for lane in lane_list)
        tempo_value = tempo_at(tempo, spawn)
        tempo_games = tempo_games_at(tempo, spawn)
        ours = [kb.tendency(c, objective) for c in draft.ours().values()]
        theirs = [kb.tendency(c, objective) for c in draft.theirs().values()]
        tendency = sum(s.value for s in ours) - sum(s.value for s in theirs)
        tendency_games = min(s.games for s in ours + theirs)
        signal = lane_value / LANE_GOLD + tempo_value / WINDOW_GOLD + tendency / TENDENCY_UNIT
        evidence = [
            Evidence(text=f"{', '.join(lane_list).title()} lane: {fmt_gold(lane_value)} gold at 10:00",
                     games=lane_games),
            Evidence(text=f"Draft tempo at {fmt_time(spawn)}: {fmt_gold(tempo_value)} gold", games=tempo_games),
            Evidence(text=f"These champions' {name} rate: {tendency * 100:+.0f} points", games=tendency_games),
        ]
        score = abs(signal) * confidence(min(e.games for e in evidence))
        if signal >= OBJECTIVE_SIGNAL:
            by = spawn - OBJECTIVE_LEAD
            items.append(ObjectiveItem(
                id=f"objective-{objective}", objective=objective, spawn_at=spawn, by=by,
                min_players=OBJECTIVE_MIN_PLAYERS, min_wards=OBJECTIVE_MIN_WARDS, evidence=evidence, score=score,
                text=(f"Contest {name} ({fmt_time(spawn)}): {OBJECTIVE_MIN_PLAYERS}+ near the pit by "
                      f"{fmt_time(by)}, {OBJECTIVE_MIN_WARDS}+ wards there."),
            ))
        elif signal <= -OBJECTIVE_SIGNAL:
            trade = [lane for lane, p in lanes.items() if p.owner == "us"] or TRADE_FALLBACK[objective]
            items.append(ConcedeItem(
                id=f"objective-{objective}", objective=objective, spawn_at=spawn, trade_lanes=trade,
                evidence=evidence, score=score,
                text=f"Give {name} ({fmt_time(spawn)}); trade for plates {' and '.join(trade)}.",
            ))
    return items


def _lane_items(lanes: dict[Lane, LanePriority]) -> list[PlanItem]:
    return [
        LaneItem(
            id=f"lane-{lane}", lane=lane, until=LANE_UNTIL,
            text=f"{lane.title()} has priority to {fmt_time(LANE_UNTIL)}: play for plates.",
            evidence=[Evidence(text=p.detail, games=p.games)],
            score=abs(p.value) / LANE_GOLD * confidence(p.games),
        )
        for lane, p in lanes.items()
        if p.owner == "us"
    ]


def _window_items(found: list[Window]) -> list[PlanItem]:
    items: list[PlanItem] = []
    for n, w in enumerate(found, start=1):
        span = f"{fmt_time(w.start)}-{fmt_time(w.end)}"
        text = (f"Our window {span}: force fights and objectives." if w.owner == "us"
                else f"Their window {span}: avoid even fights.")
        items.append(WindowItem(
            id=f"window-{n}", stance="force" if w.owner == "us" else "avoid", start=w.start, end=w.end, text=text,
            evidence=[Evidence(text=f"Draft tempo peaks at {fmt_gold(w.peak if w.owner == 'us' else -w.peak)} gold",
                               games=w.games)],
            score=w.peak / WINDOW_GOLD * confidence(w.games),
        ))
    return items


def _spike_items(kb: KnowledgeSource, draft: Draft) -> list[PlanItem]:
    items: list[PlanItem] = []
    for role in SPIKE_ROLES:
        champion = draft.theirs()[role]
        lane = ROLE_LANE[role]
        for kind in ("level6", "item1"):
            spike = kb.spike(role, champion, kind)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
            if spike is None or spike.gd_after < SPIKE_GOLD:
                continue
            if kind == "level6":
                text = f"Their {champion} hits level 6 near {fmt_time(spike.at)}: no {lane} trades without jungle near."
                what = "reaches level 6"
            else:
                text = f"Their {champion} completes a first item near {fmt_time(spike.at)}: back off {lane} until ours."
                what = "completes a first item"
            items.append(SpikeItem(
                id=f"spike-{role}-{kind}", enemy_champion=champion, role=role, kind=kind,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
                at=spike.at, text=text, score=spike.gd_after / SPIKE_GOLD * confidence(spike.games),
                evidence=[Evidence(
                    text=(f"{champion} {what} at a median {fmt_time(spike.at)}; their team gains "
                          f"{fmt_gold(spike.gd_after)} gold in the 3 minutes after"),
                    games=spike.games)],
            ))
    return items


def _side_items(lanes: dict[Lane, LanePriority]) -> list[PlanItem]:
    candidates = [lanes[lane] for lane in ("top", "bot") if lanes[lane].owner == "us"]
    if not candidates:
        return []
    best = max(candidates, key=lambda p: p.value)
    half = "top" if best.lane == "top" else "bot"
    return [SideItem(
        id="side", half=half, until=SIDE_UNTIL,
        text=f"Jungle plays {half} side to {fmt_time(SIDE_UNTIL)}, toward the {best.lane} priority.",
        evidence=[Evidence(text=best.detail, games=best.games)],
        score=best.value / LANE_GOLD * confidence(best.games) * SIDE_WEIGHT,
    )]


def build_plan(kb: KnowledgeSource, draft: Draft) -> Plan:
    tempo = draft_tempo(kb, draft)
    found = windows(tempo)
    lanes = lane_priorities(kb, draft)
    candidates = [
        *_objective_items(kb, draft, tempo, lanes),
        *_lane_items(lanes),
        *_window_items(found),
        *_spike_items(kb, draft),
        *_side_items(lanes),
    ]
    kept = [i for i in candidates if i.evidence and min(e.games for e in i.evidence) >= MIN_ITEM_GAMES]
    top = sorted(kept, key=lambda i: i.score, reverse=True)[:MAX_ITEMS]
    return Plan(draft=draft, items=sorted(top, key=item_time), tempo=tempo, windows=found,
                lanes=list(lanes.values()), scope_games=kb.scope_games)
```

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/throughline/store/app.py`, `backend/throughline/api/routes_plans.py`
- Modify: `backend/throughline/api/app.py` (app store, knowledge factory, router), `backend/throughline/store/analytics.py` (`games_with_champions`)
- Test: `backend/tests/store/test_app_store.py`, `backend/tests/api/test_plans.py`; modify `backend/tests/api/conftest.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `build_plan`, `KnowledgeBase`, `Plan`, `Draft`, `PlanItem`.
- Produces:
  - `AppStore(path)` with `create_plan(plan) -> tuple[str, str]` (id, edit token), `get_plan(id) -> Plan | None`, `update_plan(id, token, plan) -> bool`.
  - `AnalyticsStore.games_with_champions(ours: dict[Role, str], theirs: dict[Role, str]) -> list[GameSummary]`.
  - `create_app(settings, analytics=None, app_store=None, knowledge=None)` where `knowledge: Callable[[AnalyticsStore, str, datetime | None], KnowledgeSource]` defaults to `KnowledgeBase`.
  - Routes: `POST /api/plans` (body `{"draft": Draft}`), `POST /api/plans/from-game` (body `{"game_id", "us"}`), `GET /api/plans/{id}`, `PATCH /api/plans/{id}` (header `X-Edit-Token`, body `{"items": [...]}`), `GET /api/plans/{id}/games`. Create responses are `{"id", "edit_token", "plan"}`; reads are `{"id", "plan"}`.

```python
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from throughline.game.builder import DEFAULT_CHAMPIONS
from throughline.game.model import ROLES

BLUE = dict(zip(ROLES, DEFAULT_CHAMPIONS["blue"], strict=True))
RED = dict(zip(ROLES, DEFAULT_CHAMPIONS["red"], strict=True))


def test_create_read_and_edit_plan(client: TestClient) -> None:
    created = client.post("/api/plans", json={"draft": {"blue": BLUE, "red": RED, "us": "blue"}}).json()
    assert created["plan"]["draft"]["patch"] == "15.23"  # filled with the latest patch
    ids = [i["id"] for i in created["plan"]["items"]]
    assert "objective-dragon" in ids

    read = client.get(f"/api/plans/{created['id']}").json()
    assert read["plan"] == created["plan"]

    items = [i for i in created["plan"]["items"] if i["id"] != "objective-dragon"]
    items[0] = {**items[0], "text": "Edited", "origin": "edited"}
    denied = client.patch(f"/api/plans/{created['id']}", json={"items": items}, headers={"X-Edit-Token": "x"})
    assert denied.status_code == 403
    ok = client.patch(f"/api/plans/{created['id']}", json={"items": items},
                      headers={"X-Edit-Token": created["edit_token"]})
    assert ok.status_code == 200
    assert ok.json()["plan"]["items"][0]["text"] == "Edited"
    assert "objective-dragon" not in [i["id"] for i in ok.json()["plan"]["items"]]


def test_plan_from_game_and_matching_games(client: TestClient) -> None:
    created = client.post("/api/plans/from-game", json={"game_id": "g1", "us": "blue"}).json()
    assert created["plan"]["draft"]["blue"]["mid"] == "Ahri"
    games = client.get(f"/api/plans/{created['id']}/games").json()
    assert {g["game_id"] for g in games} == {"g1", "g2"}


def test_bad_requests(client: TestClient) -> None:
    assert client.get("/api/plans/missing").status_code == 404
    assert client.post("/api/plans/from-game", json={"game_id": "nope", "us": "blue"}).status_code == 404
    assert client.post("/api/plans", json={"draft": {"blue": {"top": "Aatrox"}, "red": RED, "us": "blue"}}
                       ).status_code == 422
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the app store**

`backend/throughline/store/app.py`:

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import secrets
import sqlite3
import threading
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from pathlib import Path

from throughline.plan.models import Plan

SCHEMA = """
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS plans (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, edit_token TEXT NOT NULL, created_at TEXT NOT NULL, body TEXT NOT NULL);
"""


class AppStore:
    """The app's own records. One connection, guarded by a lock."""

    def __init__(self, path: Path) -> None:
        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self._con = sqlite3.connect(str(path), check_same_thread=False)
        self._lock = threading.Lock()
        with self._lock:
            self._con.executescript(SCHEMA)

    @staticmethod
    def _new_id() -> str:
        return secrets.token_urlsafe(9)

    @staticmethod
    def _now() -> str:
        return datetime.now(UTC).isoformat()

    def create_plan(self, plan: Plan) -> tuple[str, str]:
        plan_id, token = self._new_id(), secrets.token_urlsafe(18)
        with self._lock, self._con:
            self._con.execute("INSERT INTO plans VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
                              (plan_id, token, self._now(), plan.model_dump_json()))
        return plan_id, token

    def get_plan(self, plan_id: str) -> Plan | None:
        with self._lock:
            row = self._con.execute("SELECT body FROM plans WHERE id = ?", (plan_id,)).fetchone()
        return Plan.model_validate_json(row[0]) if row else None

    def update_plan(self, plan_id: str, token: str, plan: Plan) -> bool:
        with self._lock, self._con:
            cursor = self._con.execute("UPDATE plans SET body = ? WHERE id = ? AND edit_token = ?",
                                       (plan.model_dump_json(), plan_id, token))
        return cursor.rowcount == 1
```

Add to `AnalyticsStore`:

```python
    def games_with_champions(self, ours: dict[Role, str], theirs: dict[Role, str]) -> list[GameSummary]:
        want = (sorted(ours.values()), sorted(theirs.values()))
        out = []
        for s in self.list_games(limit=100_000):
            blue, red = sorted(s.champions["blue"].values()), sorted(s.champions["red"].values())
            if want in ((blue, red), (red, blue)):
                out.append(s)
        return out
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement the routes and wire the app**

`backend/throughline/api/routes_plans.py`:

```python
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime
from typing import Any, Protocol

from fastapi import APIRouter, Header, HTTPException, Request
from pydantic import BaseModel

from throughline.api.routes_games import analytics, summary_json
from throughline.game.model import Side
from throughline.knowledge.base import KnowledgeSource
from throughline.plan.generator import build_plan
from throughline.plan.models import Draft, Plan, PlanItem, item_time
from throughline.store.analytics import AnalyticsStore
from throughline.store.app import AppStore

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api")


class KnowledgeFactory(Protocol):
    def __call__(self, store: AnalyticsStore, patch: str, before: datetime | None) -> KnowledgeSource: ...


class CreatePlan(BaseModel):
    draft: Draft


class PlanFromGame(BaseModel):
    game_id: str
    us: Side


class UpdatePlan(BaseModel):
    items: list[PlanItem]


def app_store(request: Request) -> AppStore:
    store: AppStore = request.app.state.app_store
    return store


def knowledge(request: Request, store: AnalyticsStore, patch: str, before: datetime | None) -> KnowledgeSource:
    factory: KnowledgeFactory = request.app.state.knowledge
    return factory(store, patch, before)


def _created(plan_id: str, token: str, plan: Plan) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {"id": plan_id, "edit_token": token, "plan": plan.model_dump(mode="json")}


@router.post("/plans")
def create_plan(request: Request, body: CreatePlan) -> dict[str, Any]:
    store = analytics(request)
    patch = body.draft.patch or store.latest_patch()
    if patch is None:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="no games ingested yet")
    draft = body.draft.model_copy(update={"patch": patch})
    plan = build_plan(knowledge(request, store, patch, None), draft)
    plan_id, token = app_store(request).create_plan(plan)
    return _created(plan_id, token, plan)


@router.post("/plans/from-game")
def create_plan_from_game(request: Request, body: PlanFromGame) -> dict[str, Any]:
    store = analytics(request)
    try:
        game = store.load_game(body.game_id)
    except KeyError as error:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="game not found") from error
    draft = Draft(blue=game.champions("blue"), red=game.champions("red"), us=body.us, patch=game.patch)
    # Only games played before this one: the plan the team could have had.
    plan = build_plan(knowledge(request, store, game.patch, game.meta.started_at), draft)
    plan_id, token = app_store(request).create_plan(plan)
    return {**_created(plan_id, token, plan), "game_id": game.meta.game_id}


def _get(request: Request, plan_id: str) -> Plan:
    plan = app_store(request).get_plan(plan_id)
    if plan is None:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="plan not found")
    return plan


@router.get("/plans/{plan_id}")
def get_plan(request: Request, plan_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {"id": plan_id, "plan": _get(request, plan_id).model_dump(mode="json")}


@router.patch("/plans/{plan_id}")
def update_plan(request: Request, plan_id: str, body: UpdatePlan,
                x_edit_token: str = Header(default="")) -> dict[str, Any]:
    plan = _get(request, plan_id).model_copy(update={"items": sorted(body.items, key=item_time)})
    if not app_store(request).update_plan(plan_id, x_edit_token, plan):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="this link cannot edit the plan")
    return {"id": plan_id, "plan": plan.model_dump(mode="json")}


@router.get("/plans/{plan_id}/games")
def plan_games(request: Request, plan_id: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    plan = _get(request, plan_id)
    games = analytics(request).games_with_champions(plan.draft.ours(), plan.draft.theirs())
    return [summary_json(s) for s in games]
```

In `backend/throughline/api/routes_games.py`, extract the summary dict into a helper used by `/games` and by the plans router:

```python
from throughline.store.analytics import AnalyticsStore, GameSummary


def summary_json(s: GameSummary) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {"game_id": s.game_id, "tournament": s.tournament, "started_at": s.started_at.isoformat(),
            "patch": s.patch, "teams": s.teams, "winner": s.winner, "champions": s.champions}


@router.get("/games")
def games(request: Request, q: str | None = None, limit: int = 50) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    return [summary_json(s) for s in analytics(request).list_games(q=q, limit=min(limit, 200))]
```

Replace `create_app` in `backend/throughline/api/app.py`:

```python
from __future__ import annotations

from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.middleware.gzip import GZipMiddleware

from throughline.api import routes_games, routes_plans
from throughline.api.routes_plans import KnowledgeFactory
from throughline.knowledge.base import KnowledgeBase
from throughline.settings import Settings
from throughline.store.analytics import AnalyticsStore
from throughline.store.app import AppStore


def create_app(settings: Settings, analytics: AnalyticsStore | None = None, app_store: AppStore | None = None,
               knowledge: KnowledgeFactory | None = None) -> FastAPI:
    app = FastAPI(title="Throughline")
    app.add_middleware(GZipMiddleware, minimum_size=1024)
    app.state.settings = settings
    if analytics is None:
        analytics = AnalyticsStore(settings.analytics_db)
        analytics.init_schema()
    app.state.analytics = analytics
    app.state.app_store = app_store or AppStore(settings.app_db)
    app.state.knowledge = knowledge or KnowledgeBase

    @app.get("/api/health")
    def health() -> dict[str, bool]:
        return {"ok": True}

    app.include_router(routes_games.router)
    app.include_router(routes_plans.router)
    return app
```

```ts
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { getToken, saveToken } from "./tokens";

class MemoryStorage {
  data = new Map<string, string>();
  getItem(k: string) { return this.data.get(k) ?? null; }
  setItem(k: string, v: string) { this.data.set(k, v); }
  removeItem(k: string) { this.data.delete(k); }
}

describe("tokens", () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    (globalThis as unknown as { localStorage: MemoryStorage }).localStorage = new MemoryStorage();
  });

    saveToken("plan", "a", "t1");

    expect(getToken("review", "a")).toBeUndefined();

      setItem() { throw new Error("blocked"); },
    };
    saveToken("plan", "a", "t1");

});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Types and API**

Append to `frontend/src/types.ts`:

```ts
export type Lane = "top" | "mid" | "bot";
export type Owner = "us" | "them";

export interface Draft {
  blue: Record<Role, string>;
  red: Record<Role, string>;
  us: Side;
  patch?: string | null;
}

export interface Evidence {
  text: string;
  games: number;
}

interface ItemBase {
  id: string;
  text: string;
  evidence: Evidence[];
  score: number;
  origin: "generated" | "edited" | "manual";
}

export type PlanItem =
  | (ItemBase & { type: "objective"; objective: string; spawn_at: number; by: number; min_players: number; min_wards: number })
  | (ItemBase & { type: "concede"; objective: string; spawn_at: number; trade_lanes: Lane[] })
  | (ItemBase & { type: "lane"; lane: Lane; until: number })
  | (ItemBase & { type: "window"; stance: "force" | "avoid"; start: number; end: number })
  | (ItemBase & { type: "spike"; enemy_champion: string; role: Role; kind: "level6" | "item1"; at: number })
  | (ItemBase & { type: "side"; half: "top" | "bot"; until: number });

export interface TempoPoint { minute: number; value: number; games: number }
export interface Window { start: number; end: number; owner: Owner; peak: number; games: number }
export interface LanePriority { lane: Lane; owner: Owner | null; value: number; games: number; detail: string }

export interface Plan {
  draft: Draft;
  items: PlanItem[];
  tempo: TempoPoint[];
  windows: Window[];
  lanes: LanePriority[];
  scope_games: number;
}

export interface PlanResponse {
  id: string;
  plan: Plan;
  edit_token?: string;
  game_id?: string;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Export and tokens**

`frontend/src/lib/exportPlan.ts`:

```ts
import type { Plan } from "../types";
import { ROLES } from "../types";

export function planToText(plan: Plan): string {
  const ours = plan.draft.us === "blue" ? plan.draft.blue : plan.draft.red;
  const theirs = plan.draft.us === "blue" ? plan.draft.red : plan.draft.blue;
  const lines = [
    `**Game plan** (patch ${plan.draft.patch ?? "?"}, ${plan.scope_games} games)`,
    `Us: ${ROLES.map((r) => ours[r]).join(", ")}`,
    `Them: ${ROLES.map((r) => theirs[r]).join(", ")}`,
    "",
  ];
  for (const item of plan.items) {
    const games = item.evidence.length ? Math.min(...item.evidence.map((e) => e.games)) : 0;
    lines.push(`- ${item.text}${games ? ` (${games} games)` : ""}`);
  }
  return lines.join("\n");
}
```

`frontend/src/lib/tokens.ts`:

```ts
type Kind = "plan" | "review";
const key = (kind: Kind, id: string) => `throughline:${kind}:${id}`;

export function saveToken(kind: Kind, id: string, token: string): void {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(key(kind, id), token);
  } catch {
    // Storage blocked: the edit link in the URL still works for this visit.
  }
}

export function getToken(kind: Kind, id: string): string | undefined {
  try {
    return localStorage.getItem(key(kind, id)) ?? undefined;
  } catch {
    return undefined;
  }
}

export function takeTokenFromHash(kind: Kind, id: string): void {
  const match = window.location.hash.match(/t=([\w-]+)/);
  if (!match) return;
  saveToken(kind, id, match[1]);
  history.replaceState(null, "", window.location.pathname + window.location.search);
}
```

```tsx
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { api } from "../api";
import { useDdragon } from "../lib/ddragon";

interface Props {
  label: string;
  value: string;
  onChange: (value: string) => void;
}

export function ChampionPicker({ label, value, onChange }: Props) {
  const { data = [] } = useQuery({ queryKey: ["champions"], queryFn: api.champions, staleTime: Infinity });
  const { iconFor } = useDdragon();
  const icon = value ? iconFor(value) : undefined;
  return (
    <label className="flex items-center gap-2">
      <span className="w-16 text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-500">{label}</span>
      {icon ? <img src={icon} alt="" className="h-8 w-8 rounded" /> : <span className="h-8 w-8 rounded bg-zinc-800" />}
      <input
        list="champion-list"
        value={value}
        onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
        className="min-w-0 flex-1 rounded bg-zinc-900 px-2 py-1"
        aria-label={label}
      />
      <datalist id="champion-list">
        {data.map((c) => (
          <option key={c.name} value={c.name}>{`${c.games} games`}</option>
        ))}
      </datalist>
    </label>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/components/DraftStrip.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useDdragon } from "../lib/ddragon";
import { ROLES, type Draft, type Side } from "../types";

export function DraftStrip({ draft }: { draft: Draft }) {
  const { iconFor } = useDdragon();
  const row = (side: Side) => (
    <div className={`flex items-center gap-2 ${draft.us === side ? "" : "opacity-80"}`}>
      <span className={`w-12 text-xs font-medium ${side === "blue" ? "text-blue-side" : "text-red-side"}`}>
        {draft.us === side ? "Us" : "Them"}
      </span>
      {ROLES.map((role) => {
        const champion = draft[side][role];
        const icon = iconFor(champion);
        return icon ? (
          <img key={role} src={icon} alt={champion} title={`${role}: ${champion}`} className="h-10 w-10 rounded" />
        ) : (
          <span key={role} className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded bg-zinc-800 text-[10px]">{champion}</span>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
      {row("blue")}
      {row("red")}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/components/PlanItemCard.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import type { PlanItem } from "../types";

const LABEL: Record<PlanItem["type"], string> = {
  objective: "Objective", concede: "Concede", lane: "Lane", window: "Window", spike: "Spike", side: "Jungle side",
};

interface Props {
  item: PlanItem;
  editable: boolean;
  onChange: (item: PlanItem) => void;
  onRemove: () => void;
}

export function PlanItemCard({ item, editable, onChange, onRemove }: Props) {
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(false);
  const [text, setText] = useState(item.text);
  const games = item.evidence.length ? Math.min(...item.evidence.map((e) => e.games)) : 0;
  return (
    <li className="rounded-lg border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900/60 p-4">
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
        <span className="rounded bg-zinc-800 px-2 py-0.5 text-xs text-zinc-300">{LABEL[item.type]}</span>
        <span className="text-xs tabular-nums text-zinc-500">{games} games</span>
      </div>
      {editing ? (
        <div className="mt-2 flex gap-2">
          <input value={text} onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value)} className="flex-1 rounded bg-zinc-950 px-2 py-1" />
          <button
            type="button"
            className="rounded bg-zinc-100 px-3 py-1 text-sm text-zinc-900"
            onClick={() => {
              onChange({ ...item, text, origin: "edited" });
              setEditing(false);
            }}
          >
            Save
          </button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <p className="mt-2 font-medium">{item.text}</p>
      )}
      <ul className="mt-2 space-y-1 text-sm text-zinc-400">
        {item.evidence.map((e) => (
          <li key={e.text}>{e.text} <span className="text-zinc-600">({e.games} games)</span></li>
        ))}
      </ul>
      {item.origin !== "generated" && <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-amber-400">Edited by the coach</p>}
      {editable && !editing && (
        <div className="mt-3 flex gap-3 text-sm text-zinc-400">
          <button type="button" className="hover:text-zinc-100" onClick={() => setEditing(true)}>Edit</button>
          <button type="button" className="hover:text-rose-300" onClick={onRemove}>Remove</button>
        </div>
      )}
    </li>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/pages/DraftPage.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useMutation } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useState } from "react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { api } from "../api";
import { ChampionPicker } from "../components/ChampionPicker";
import { saveToken } from "../lib/tokens";
import { ROLES, type Role, type Side } from "../types";

const empty = (): Record<Role, string> => ({ top: "", jungle: "", mid: "", bot: "", support: "" });

export function DraftPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [blue, setBlue] = useState(empty);
  const [red, setRed] = useState(empty);
  const [us, setUs] = useState<Side>("blue");
  const create = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => api.createPlan({ blue, red, us }),
    onSuccess: (res) => {
      if (res.edit_token) saveToken("plan", res.id, res.edit_token);
      navigate(`/plan/${res.id}`);
    },
  });
  const complete = ROLES.every((r) => blue[r] && red[r]);
  const column = (side: Side, value: Record<Role, string>, set: (v: Record<Role, string>) => void) => (
    <fieldset className="flex flex-1 flex-col gap-2">
      <legend className={`mb-2 font-medium ${side === "blue" ? "text-blue-side" : "text-red-side"}`}>
        {side === "blue" ? "Blue side" : "Red side"}
      </legend>
      {ROLES.map((role) => (
        <ChampionPicker key={role} label={role} value={value[role]} onChange={(v) => set({ ...value, [role]: v })} />
      ))}
    </fieldset>
  );
  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-4xl px-4 py-8">
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold">New draft</h1>
      <div className="mt-6 flex flex-col gap-8 md:flex-row">
        {column("blue", blue, setBlue)}
        {column("red", red, setRed)}
      </div>
      <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4">
        <span className="text-sm text-zinc-400">We are</span>
        {(["blue", "red"] as Side[]).map((side) => (
          <button
            key={side}
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setUs(side)}
            className={`rounded px-3 py-1 text-sm ${us === side ? "bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-900" : "bg-zinc-800"}`}
          >
            {side === "blue" ? "Blue" : "Red"}
          </button>
        ))}
        <button
          type="button"
          disabled={!complete || create.isPending}
          onClick={() => create.mutate()}
          className="ml-auto rounded bg-emerald-500 px-4 py-2 font-medium text-zinc-950 disabled:opacity-40"
        >
          {create.isPending ? "Building the plan..." : "Build the plan"}
        </button>
      </div>
      {create.error && <p className="mt-4 text-rose-400">{String(create.error.message)}</p>}
    </main>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/pages/PlanFromGamePage.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useEffect } from "react";
import { useNavigate, useParams, useSearchParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { api } from "../api";
import { saveToken } from "../lib/tokens";
import type { Side } from "../types";

export function PlanFromGamePage() {
  const { gameId = "" } = useParams();
  const [params] = useSearchParams();
  const us = (params.get("us") === "red" ? "red" : "blue") as Side;
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { data, error } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["plan-from-game", gameId, us],
    queryFn: () => api.planFromGame(gameId, us),
    staleTime: Infinity,
  });
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!data) return;
    if (data.edit_token) saveToken("plan", data.id, data.edit_token);
    navigate(`/plan/${data.id}?game=${encodeURIComponent(gameId)}`, { replace: true });
  }, [data, gameId, navigate]);
  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-4xl px-4 py-8 text-zinc-400">
      {error ? <p className="text-rose-400">Could not build the plan: {String(error.message)}</p> : "Building the plan..."}
    </main>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/pages/PlanPage.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { api } from "../api";
import { DraftStrip } from "../components/DraftStrip";
import { PlanItemCard } from "../components/PlanItemCard";
import { planToText } from "../lib/exportPlan";
import { getToken, takeTokenFromHash } from "../lib/tokens";
import type { PlanItem } from "../types";

export function PlanPage() {
  const { id = "" } = useParams();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState<string | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => takeTokenFromHash("plan", id), [id]);
  const token = getToken("plan", id);
  const { data, error } = useQuery({ queryKey: ["plan", id], queryFn: () => api.plan(id) });
  const save = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (items: PlanItem[]) => api.updatePlan(id, token ?? "", items),
    onSuccess: (res) => queryClient.setQueryData(["plan", id], res),
  });

  if (error) return <main className="mx-auto max-w-4xl px-4 py-8 text-rose-400">Plan not found.</main>;
  if (!data) return <main className="mx-auto max-w-4xl px-4 py-8 text-zinc-500">Loading the plan...</main>;
  const { plan } = data;

  const copy = async (label: string, text: string) => {
    await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
    setCopied(label);
    setTimeout(() => setCopied(null), 1500);
  };

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-4xl px-4 py-8">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-6">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold">Game plan</h1>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-zinc-500">Patch {plan.draft.patch} · built from {plan.scope_games} pro games</p>
        </div>
        <DraftStrip draft={plan.draft} />
      </div>
      <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap gap-2 text-sm">
        <button type="button" className="rounded bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-1" onClick={() => copy("discord", planToText(plan))}>
          {copied === "discord" ? "Copied" : "Copy for Discord"}
        </button>
        <button type="button" className="rounded bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-1"
          onClick={() => copy("link", `${window.location.origin}/plan/${id}`)}>
          {copied === "link" ? "Copied" : "Copy read-only link"}
        </button>
        {token && (
          <button type="button" className="rounded bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-1"
            onClick={() => copy("edit", `${window.location.origin}/plan/${id}#t=${token}`)}>
            {copied === "edit" ? "Copied" : "Copy edit link"}
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
      {plan.items.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="mt-8 text-zinc-400">Not enough games behind this draft for a plan. Try an earlier patch or a more common draft.</p>
      ) : (
        <ul className="mt-8 space-y-3">
          {plan.items.map((item) => (
            <PlanItemCard
              key={item.id}
              item={item}
              editable={Boolean(token)}
              onChange={(next) => save.mutate(plan.items.map((i) => (i.id === next.id ? next : i)))}
              onRemove={() => save.mutate(plan.items.filter((i) => i.id !== item.id))}
            />
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`frontend/src/components/tempoScale.ts`:

```ts
import type { TempoPoint } from "../types";

export const CHART_SECONDS = 1800;
const GOLD_STEP = 250;

export function tempoScale(points: TempoPoint[], width: number, height: number) {
  const peak = Math.max(GOLD_STEP, ...points.map((p) => Math.abs(p.value)));
  const maxGold = Math.ceil(peak / GOLD_STEP) * GOLD_STEP;
  return {
    maxGold,
    x: (seconds: number) => (Math.min(seconds, CHART_SECONDS) / CHART_SECONDS) * width,
    y: (gold: number) => height / 2 - (gold / maxGold) * (height / 2),
  };
}
```

`frontend/src/components/TempoChart.tsx`:

```tsx
import { fmtTime } from "../lib/time";
import type { Plan } from "../types";
import { CHART_SECONDS, tempoScale } from "./tempoScale";

const W = 640;
const H = 180;

export function TempoChart({ plan }: { plan: Plan }) {
  const s = tempoScale(plan.tempo, W, H);
  const path = [{ minute: 0, value: 0 }, ...plan.tempo]
    .map((p, i) => `${i === 0 ? "M" : "L"}${s.x(p.minute * 60).toFixed(1)},${s.y(p.value).toFixed(1)}`)
    .join(" ");
  const marks = plan.items.flatMap((item) =>
    item.type === "spike" ? [{ t: item.at, label: `${item.enemy_champion} ${item.kind === "level6" ? "6" : "item"}` }]
    : item.type === "objective" || item.type === "concede" ? [{ t: item.spawn_at, label: item.objective }]
    : [],
  );
  return (
    <figure className="w-full">
      <svg viewBox={`0 0 ${W} ${H + 24}`} className="w-full" role="img" aria-label="Draft tempo from 0 to 30 minutes">
        {plan.windows.map((w) => (
          <rect key={w.start} x={s.x(w.start)} y={0} width={s.x(w.end) - s.x(w.start)} height={H}
            className={w.owner === "us" ? "fill-emerald-500/15" : "fill-rose-500/15"} />
        ))}
        <line x1={0} x2={W} y1={s.y(0)} y2={s.y(0)} className="stroke-zinc-700" />
        <path d={path} fill="none" className="stroke-zinc-100" strokeWidth={2} />
        {marks.map((m) => (
          <g key={`${m.t}-${m.label}`}>
            <line x1={s.x(m.t)} x2={s.x(m.t)} y1={0} y2={H} className="stroke-zinc-600" strokeDasharray="3 3" />
            <text x={s.x(m.t) + 3} y={12} className="fill-zinc-400 text-[10px]">{m.label}</text>
          </g>
        ))}
        {[0, 600, 1200, CHART_SECONDS].map((t) => (
          <text key={t} x={Math.min(s.x(t), W - 24)} y={H + 16} className="fill-zinc-500 text-[10px]">{fmtTime(t)}</text>
        ))}
        <text x={4} y={s.y(s.maxGold) + 12} className="fill-zinc-500 text-[10px]">+{s.maxGold} us</text>
        <text x={4} y={s.y(-s.maxGold) - 4} className="fill-zinc-500 text-[10px]">-{s.maxGold} them</text>
      </svg>
      <figcaption className="mt-1 text-xs text-zinc-500">
        Expected gold difference from the draft alone. Green: our window. Red: theirs.
      </figcaption>
    </figure>
  );
}
```

```python
from throughline.game.builder import GameBuilder
from throughline.plan.models import ConcedeItem, LaneItem, ObjectiveItem, SideItem, SpikeItem, WindowItem
from throughline.review.checks import check_item
from throughline.review.context import ReviewContext

PIT = (9866.0, 4414.0)


def dragon_item() -> ObjectiveItem:
    return ObjectiveItem(id="objective-dragon", text="", objective="dragon", spawn_at=300.0, by=280.0)


def grouped_at_dragon(builder: GameBuilder) -> GameBuilder:
    for pid in (1, 2, 3):
        builder.walk(pid, 200.0, 260.0, (6000.0, 2000.0), PIT)
    return builder


def test_objective_followed() -> None:
    game = (grouped_at_dragon(GameBuilder(duration=1200.0))
            .ward(230.0, 5, 9500.0, 4600.0).ward(240.0, 4, 9300.0, 4000.0)
            .objective(320.0, "dragon", "blue").build())

    assert check.status == "followed"

    assert check.at == 280.0


def test_objective_partly_without_wards_and_red_view() -> None:
    game = grouped_at_dragon(GameBuilder(duration=1200.0)).objective(320.0, "dragon", "blue").build()
    assert check_item(dragon_item(), ReviewContext.build(game, "blue")).status == "partly"
    red = check_item(dragon_item(), ReviewContext.build(game, "red"))
    assert red.status == "not_followed"
    assert red.numbers.endswith("they took it at 5:20")


def test_objective_not_applicable_when_game_ends_first() -> None:
    game = GameBuilder(duration=250.0).build()
    assert check_item(dragon_item(), ReviewContext.build(game, "blue")).status == "not_applicable"


def test_concede_followed_with_a_trade() -> None:
    item = ConcedeItem(id="objective-dragon", text="", objective="dragon", spawn_at=300.0, trade_lanes=["top"])
    game = (GameBuilder(duration=1200.0)
            .walk(8, 250.0, 290.0, (12000.0, 3000.0), PIT)
            .plate(310.0, "top", "blue")
            .objective(330.0, "dragon", "red").build())
    check = check_item(item, ReviewContext.build(game, "blue"))
    assert check.status == "followed"
    assert check.numbers == "0 of 5 at the pit at 5:00; 1 plate or structure taken in top meanwhile"


def test_lane_followed_and_partly() -> None:
    item = LaneItem(id="lane-bot", text="", lane="bot", until=480.0)
    ahead = (GameBuilder(duration=1200.0).gold_lead("blue", 1000, 0.0, 400.0)
             .plate(400.0, "bot", "blue").plate(450.0, "bot", "blue").build())
    check = check_item(item, ReviewContext.build(ahead, "blue"))
    assert check.status == "followed"
    assert check.numbers == "Bot gold +400 at 8:00; plates 2-0"
    behind_on_plates = (GameBuilder(duration=1200.0).gold_lead("blue", 1000, 0.0, 400.0)
                        .plate(400.0, "bot", "red").build())
    assert check_item(item, ReviewContext.build(behind_on_plates, "blue")).status == "partly"


def fight(builder: GameBuilder, t: float, blue: tuple[int, ...], red: tuple[int, ...],
          kills: list[tuple[int, int]]) -> GameBuilder:
    for pid in blue + red:
        builder.walk(pid, t - 10.0, t + 5.0, (5000.0, 5000.0), (5000.0, 5000.0))
    for n, (killer, victim) in enumerate(kills):
        builder.kill(t + n, killer, victim, 5000.0, 5000.0)
    return builder


def test_window_force_and_avoid() -> None:
    force = WindowItem(id="window-1", text="", stance="force", start=900.0, end=1200.0)
    won = fight(GameBuilder(duration=1500.0), 1000.0, (1, 2, 3), (6, 7), [(1, 6), (2, 7)]).build()
    check = check_item(force, ReviewContext.build(won, "blue"))
    assert check.status == "followed"
    assert check.numbers == "1 fight from 15:00 to 20:00: won 1, lost 0"
    assert check_item(force, ReviewContext.build(GameBuilder(duration=1500.0).build(), "blue")).status == "not_followed"

    avoid = WindowItem(id="window-2", text="", stance="avoid", start=900.0, end=1200.0)
    lost_even = fight(GameBuilder(duration=1500.0), 1000.0, (1, 2), (6, 7, 8), [(6, 1), (7, 2)]).build()
    assert check_item(avoid, ReviewContext.build(lost_even, "blue")).status == "not_followed"
    assert check_item(avoid, ReviewContext.build(GameBuilder(duration=1500.0).build(), "blue")).status == "followed"


def test_spike_and_side() -> None:
    spike = SpikeItem(id="spike-mid-level6", text="", enemy_champion="Orianna", role="mid", kind="level6", at=380.0)
    died = GameBuilder(duration=1200.0).kill(420.0, 8, 3, 7500.0, 7500.0).build()
    check = check_item(spike, ReviewContext.build(died, "blue"))
    assert (check.status, check.at) == ("not_followed", 420.0)
    assert check.numbers == "1 death in mid from 6:20 to 8:20"
    assert check_item(spike, ReviewContext.build(GameBuilder(duration=1200.0).build(), "blue")).status == "followed"

    side = SideItem(id="side", text="", half="top", until=360.0)
    topside = GameBuilder(duration=1200.0).walk(2, 90.0, 360.0, (3000.0, 9000.0), (4000.0, 11000.0)).build()
    check = check_item(side, ReviewContext.build(topside, "blue"))
    assert check.status == "followed"
    assert check.numbers == "Jungler spent 100% of 1:30-6:00 on the top side"
```
